107: WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE = 0x031D
108: _WM_RENDERFORMAT = 0x0305
109: _WM_RENDERALLFORMATS = 0x0306
110: 
111: def _pump_messages_except_clipboard_render():
112:     """Bơm message khi đang chờ tải file, nhưng không Dispatch WM_RENDERFORMAT.
113: 
114:     PeekMessage(hwnd=0) + Dispatch sẽ gọi lồng WM_RENDERFORMAT. Handler trùng
115:     return 0 không SetClipboardData → Explorer Win11 dán PE dở, không hiện dialog.
116:     Để RENDERFORMAT nằm lại queue, xử lý sau khi lần chờ hiện tại kết thúc.
117:     """
118:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
119:     msg = wintypes.MSG()
120:     PM_REMOVE = 1
121:     pumped = False
122:     while user32.PeekMessageW(ctypes.byref(msg), 0, 0, _WM_RENDERFORMAT - 1, PM_REMOVE):
123:         user32.TranslateMessage(ctypes.byref(msg))
124:         user32.DispatchMessageW(ctypes.byref(msg))
125:         pumped = True
126:     while user32.PeekMessageW(
127:         ctypes.byref(msg), 0, _WM_RENDERALLFORMATS + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF, PM_REMOVE
128:     ):
129:         user32.TranslateMessage(ctypes.byref(msg))
130:         user32.DispatchMessageW(ctypes.byref(msg))
131:         pumped = True
132:     return pumped

140: def _xfer_staging_dir(base_dir, xfer_id):
141:     if not base_dir:
142:         return base_dir
143:     if xfer_id is None:
144:         return base_dir
145:     return os.path.join(base_dir, f".rdxfer_{xfer_id}")

148: def _hide_win_path(path):
149:     """Ẩn thư mục tạm trên Windows (dấu chấm không ẩn Explorer)."""
150:     if sys.platform != "win32" or not path:
151:         return
152:     try:
153:         GetFileAttributesW = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetFileAttributesW
154:         SetFileAttributesW = ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetFileAttributesW
155:         GetFileAttributesW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPCWSTR]
156:         GetFileAttributesW.restype = wintypes.DWORD
157:         SetFileAttributesW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPCWSTR, wintypes.DWORD]
158:         attrs = GetFileAttributesW(str(path))
159:         if attrs == 0xFFFFFFFF:
160:             return
161:         SetFileAttributesW(str(path), int(attrs) | 0x02 | 0x04)  # HIDDEN | SYSTEM
162:     except Exception:
163:         pass

166: def _purge_rdxfer_staging(dest_dir=None, xfer_id=None, extra_paths=None, force_all=False):
167:     """Xóa .rdxfer_* sau khi file đã ra thư mục đích — không để lại folder tạm."""
168:     targets = []
169:     if dest_dir and xfer_id is not None:
170:         targets.append(_xfer_staging_dir(dest_dir, xfer_id))
171:     for p in extra_paths or []:
172:         if not p:
173:             continue
174:         try:
175:             ap = os.path.abspath(p)
176:             if os.path.basename(ap).startswith(".rdxfer_"):
177:                 targets.append(ap)
178:                 continue
179:             cur = ap if os.path.isdir(ap) else os.path.dirname(ap)
180:             for _ in range(10):
181:                 if not cur:
182:                     break
183:                 if os.path.basename(cur).startswith(".rdxfer_"):
184:                     targets.append(cur)
185:                     break
186:                 parent = os.path.dirname(cur)
187:                 if not parent or parent == cur:
188:                     break
189:                 cur = parent
190:         except Exception:
191:             pass
192:     if dest_dir and os.path.isdir(dest_dir):
193:         try:
194:             want = f".rdxfer_{int(xfer_id)}" if xfer_id is not None else None
195:             for name in os.listdir(dest_dir):
196:                 if not name.startswith(".rdxfer_"):
197:                     continue
198:                 p = os.path.join(dest_dir, name)
199:                 if force_all or (want and name == want):
200:                     targets.append(p)
201:                     continue
202:                 # Không đúng xfer này: chỉ xóa nếu rỗng (sót sau paste).
203:                 try:
204:                     if not os.listdir(p):
205:                         targets.append(p)
206:                 except Exception:
207:                     pass
208:         except Exception:
209:             pass
210:     seen = set()
211:     for t in targets:
212:         try:
213:             key = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(t))
214:         except Exception:
215:             continue
216:         if key in seen:
217:             continue
218:         seen.add(key)
219:         if not os.path.basename(t).startswith(".rdxfer_"):
220:             continue
221:         _retry_remove_path(t, retries=12)
222:         if os.path.lexists(t):
223:             try:
224:                 threading.Timer(0.6, lambda p=t: _retry_remove_path(p, retries=8)).start()
225:                 threading.Timer(2.0, lambda p=t: _retry_remove_path(p, retries=8)).start()
226:             except Exception:
227:                 pass

230: def _is_transfer_staging_dir(path):
231:     if not path:
232:         return False
233:     abs_path = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(path))
234:     staging = [
235:         os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(HEADLESS_TRANSFER_DIR)),
236:         os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.environ.get("TEMP", os.path.expanduser("~")), "RemoteDesktopTransfers"))),
237:     ]
238:     if abs_path in staging:
239:         return True
240:     base = os.path.basename(abs_path)
241:     return base.startswith(".rdxfer_")

244: def _file_offer_fp(files):
245:     """Vân tay danh sách file copy — để nhận copy mới dù offer_id bị lặp/reset."""
246:     parts = []
247:     for f in files or []:
248:         if isinstance(f, dict):
249:             name = os.path.basename(str(f.get("name") or "").replace("\\", "/"))
250:             path = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(str(f.get("path") or ""))) if f.get("path") else ""
251:             try:
252:                 size = int(f.get("size") or 0)
253:             except Exception:
254:                 size = 0
255:             parts.append((name, size, path))
256:         else:
257:             p = str(f or "")
258:             parts.append((os.path.basename(p.replace("\\", "/")), 0, os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(p)) if p else ""))
259:     return tuple(parts)

262: def _rel_copy_name(item):
263:     if isinstance(item, dict):
264:         raw = str(item.get("name") or "")
265:     else:
266:         raw = str(item or "")
267:     return raw.replace("\\", "/").strip("/")

270: def _copy_batch_kind_and_name(files, fallback=""):
271:     """Paste file → ('file', tên file); paste thư mục → ('folder', tên folder)."""
272:     files = files or []
273:     names = []
274:     for item in files:
275:         name = _rel_copy_name(item)
276:         if name:
277:             names.append(name)
278:     if not names:
279:         raw = fallback or _("Tệp tin")
280:         return "file", os.path.basename(str(raw).replace("\\", "/")) or raw
281: 
282:     folder_tops = []
283:     seen_folders = set()
284:     loose = []
285:     for name in names:
286:         parts = [p for p in name.split("/") if p]
287:         if len(parts) >= 2:
288:             top = parts[0]
289:             if top not in seen_folders:
290:                 seen_folders.add(top)
291:                 folder_tops.append(top)
292:         else:
293:             loose.append(parts[0] if parts else name)
294: 
295:     if folder_tops and not loose:
296:         label = folder_tops[0]
297:         extra = len(folder_tops) - 1
298:         if extra > 0:
299:             label += _(" và {count} mục khác").format(count=extra)
300:         return "folder", label
301: 
302:     label = os.path.basename(names[0]) or names[0]
303:     extra = len(names) - 1
304:     if extra > 0:
305:         label += _(" và {count} mục khác").format(count=extra)
306:     return "file", label

309: def _localized_batch_name(files, fallback=""):
310:     _kind, name = _copy_batch_kind_and_name(files, fallback)
311:     return name

314: def _same_volume(path_a, path_b):
315:     try:
316:         return os.path.splitdrive(os.path.abspath(path_a))[0].lower() == os.path.splitdrive(os.path.abspath(path_b))[0].lower()
317:     except Exception:
318:         return False

321: def _path_byte_size(path):
322:     try:
323:         if os.path.isfile(path):
324:             return os.path.getsize(path)
325:         total = 0
326:         for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(path):
327:             for name in files:
328:                 try:
329:                     total += os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, name))
330:                 except Exception:
331:                     pass
332:         return total
333:     except Exception:
334:         return 0

337: def _copy_file_with_progress(src, dst, on_progress=None, should_stop=None):
338:     import shutil
339:     wrote = 0
340:     completed = False
341:     try:
342:         with open(src, "rb") as inf, open(dst, "wb") as outf:
343:             while True:
344:                 if should_stop and should_stop():
345:                     raise InterruptedError("cancel")
346:                 buf = inf.read(1024 * 1024)
347:                 if not buf:
348:                     break
349:                 outf.write(buf)
350:                 wrote += len(buf)
351:                 if on_progress:
352:                     on_progress(len(buf))
353:         try:
354:             shutil.copystat(src, dst, follow_symlinks=True)
355:         except Exception:
356:             pass
357:         completed = True
358:         try:
359:             os.remove(src)
360:         except Exception:
361:             threading.Thread(target=_retry_remove_path, args=(src,), daemon=True).start()
362:     except Exception:
363:         if not completed:
364:             try:
365:                 _retry_remove_path(dst)
366:             except Exception:
367:                 pass
368:         raise

374: def relocate_transfer_files(src_paths, dest_dir, on_progress=None, should_stop=None):
375:     """Chuyển file từ thư mục tạm sang thư mục Explorer đang Paste. Trả về (paths, moved_all)."""
376:     with _file_io_lock:
377:         return _relocate_transfer_files_unlocked(src_paths, dest_dir, on_progress, should_stop)
378: 
379: 
380: def _relocate_transfer_files_unlocked(src_paths, dest_dir, on_progress=None, should_stop=None, replace_keys=None):
381:     """Chuyển file từ thư mục tạm sang thư mục Explorer đang Paste. Trả về (paths, moved_all).
382: 
383:     replace_keys: set đường dẫn đích (normcase abspath) được phép ghi đè.
384:     Nếu đích đã có file thật mà không nằm trong replace_keys → KHÔNG xóa/ghi đè.
385:     """
386:     import shutil
387:     if not dest_dir or not os.path.isdir(dest_dir) or _is_transfer_staging_dir(dest_dir):
388:         return list(src_paths or []), False
389:     allow_replace = set()
390:     for k in (replace_keys or []):
391:         try:
392:             allow_replace.add(os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(k)))
393:         except Exception:
394:             allow_replace.add(k)
395:     dest_abs = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(dest_dir))
396:     moved = []
397:     all_in_dest = True
398:     for src in src_paths or []:
399:         if should_stop and should_stop():
400:             all_in_dest = False
401:             break
402:         if not src or not os.path.exists(src):
403:             continue
404:         src_abs = os.path.abspath(src)
405:         if os.path.basename(src_abs).startswith(".rdxfer_") or _is_transfer_staging_dir(src_abs):
406:             continue
407:         if _is_probe_stub_name(src_abs):
408:             continue
409:         src_dir = os.path.normcase(os.path.dirname(src_abs))
410:         if src_dir == dest_abs:
411:             moved.append(src_abs)
412:             if on_progress:
413:                 on_progress(_path_byte_size(src_abs))
414:             continue
415:         dest_path = os.path.join(dest_dir, os.path.basename(src_abs))
416:         try:
417:             dest_key = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(dest_path))
418:         except Exception:
419:             dest_key = dest_path
420:         try:
421:             if should_stop and should_stop():
422:                 raise InterruptedError("cancel")
423:             # Folder paste: đích đã có file chuyển sớm — gộp, không rmtree xóa file đã xong.
424:             if os.path.isdir(src_abs) and os.path.isdir(dest_path):
425:                 child_ok = True
426:                 try:
427:                     names = os.listdir(src_abs)
428:                 except Exception:
429:                     names = []
430:                 for name in names:
431:                     child = os.path.join(src_abs, name)
432:                     _nested, ok = _relocate_transfer_files_unlocked(
433:                         [child], dest_path, on_progress, should_stop, replace_keys=allow_replace
434:                     )
435:                     if not ok:
436:                         child_ok = False
437:                 if child_ok:
438:                     try:
439:                         os.rmdir(src_abs)
440:                     except Exception:
441:                         pass
442:                 else:
443:                     all_in_dest = False
444:                 moved.append(os.path.abspath(dest_path))
445:                 continue
446:             if os.path.exists(dest_path):
447:                 if not _is_transfer_junk_at_dest(dest_path) and dest_key not in allow_replace:
448:                     log_debug(f"[relocate] Bo ghi de (chua hoi Replace): {dest_path}")
449:                     # Giữ bản staging — caller (agent) sẽ relocate sau khi có replace_keys.
450:                     moved.append(src_abs)
451:                     all_in_dest = False
452:                     continue
453:                 if os.path.isdir(dest_path) and not os.path.islink(dest_path):
454:                     shutil.rmtree(dest_path, ignore_errors=True)
455:                 else:
456:                     os.remove(dest_path)
457:             if os.path.isdir(src_abs) or _same_volume(src_abs, dest_path):
458:                 shutil.move(src_abs, dest_path)
459:                 if on_progress:
460:                     on_progress(_path_byte_size(dest_path))
461:             else:
462:                 _copy_file_with_progress(src_abs, dest_path, on_progress, should_stop=should_stop)
463:             moved.append(os.path.abspath(dest_path))
464:         except InterruptedError:
465:             _retry_remove_path(dest_path)
466:             all_in_dest = False
467:             break
468:         except Exception as e:
469:             log_debug(f"[relocate_transfer_files] move failed {src_abs} -> {dest_path}: {e}")
470:             try:
471:                 if should_stop and should_stop():
472:                     raise InterruptedError("cancel")
473:                 if os.path.exists(dest_path) and not _is_transfer_junk_at_dest(dest_path) and dest_key not in allow_replace:
474:                     raise RuntimeError("dest exists, no replace")
475:                 if os.path.isdir(src_abs):
476:                     shutil.copytree(src_abs, dest_path)
477:                     shutil.rmtree(src_abs, ignore_errors=True)
478:                     if on_progress:
479:                         on_progress(_path_byte_size(dest_path))
480:                 else:
481:                     _copy_file_with_progress(src_abs, dest_path, on_progress, should_stop=should_stop)
482:                 moved.append(os.path.abspath(dest_path))
483:             except InterruptedError:
484:                 _retry_remove_path(dest_path)
485:                 all_in_dest = False
486:                 break
487:             except Exception as e2:
488:                 log_debug(f"[relocate_transfer_files] copy fallback failed: {e2}")
489:                 _retry_remove_path(dest_path)
490:                 moved.append(src_abs)
491:                 all_in_dest = False
492:     if moved and all_in_dest:
493:         for p in moved:
494:             if os.path.normcase(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(p))) != dest_abs:
495:                 all_in_dest = False
496:                 break
497:     return moved, bool(moved) and all_in_dest

500: def _promote_completed_xfer_file(staging_path, dest_dir, rel_name, replace_keys=None):
501:     """Đưa 1 file đã tải xong từ .rdxfer_* ra thư mục đích (giữ cây thư mục). Rename cùng ổ — không copy 4GB."""
502:     import shutil
503:     if not staging_path or not dest_dir or not os.path.isfile(staging_path):
504:         return staging_path
505:     if _is_transfer_staging_dir(dest_dir) or not os.path.isdir(dest_dir):
506:         return staging_path
507:     rel = str(rel_name or os.path.basename(staging_path)).replace("/", os.sep).replace("\\", os.sep)
508:     rel = rel.lstrip("\\/")
509:     if not rel:
510:         rel = os.path.basename(staging_path)
511:     dest_path = os.path.join(dest_dir, rel)
512:     try:
513:         if os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(staging_path)) == os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(dest_path)):
514:             return dest_path
515:     except Exception:
516:         pass
517:     parent = os.path.dirname(dest_path)
518:     if parent:
519:         os.makedirs(parent, exist_ok=True)
520:     allow_replace = set()
521:     for k in (replace_keys or []):
522:         try:
523:             allow_replace.add(os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(k)))
524:         except Exception:
525:             allow_replace.add(k)
526:     if os.path.exists(dest_path):
527:         try:
528:             dest_key = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(dest_path))
529:         except Exception:
530:             dest_key = dest_path
531:         top_key = None
532:         try:
533:             top = rel.replace("\\", "/").strip("/").split("/")[0]
534:             top_key = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(os.path.join(dest_dir, top)))
535:         except Exception:
536:             pass
537:         may_replace = dest_key in allow_replace or (top_key and top_key in allow_replace)
538:         # Không im lặng ghi đè file user — chỉ xóa stub/junk; file thật cần replace_keys
539:         # (user đã chọn Replace trên ClassicCopyDialog).
540:         if not _is_transfer_junk_at_dest(dest_path) and not may_replace:
541:             log_debug(f"[promote] Giữ file đích đã có, không ghi đè: {dest_path}")
542:             try:
543:                 log_overwrite_conflict(
544:                     f"PROMOTE keep existing (no overwrite): {dest_path} "
545:                     f"staging={staging_path}"
546:                 )
547:             except Exception:
548:                 pass
549:             return staging_path
550:         try:
551:             log_overwrite_conflict(
552:                 f"PROMOTE remove junk then write: {dest_path}"
553:             )
554:             if os.path.isdir(dest_path) and not os.path.islink(dest_path):
555:                 shutil.rmtree(dest_path, ignore_errors=True)
556:             else:
557:                 os.remove(dest_path)
558:         except Exception:
559:             _retry_remove_path(dest_path)
560:     try:
561:         os.replace(staging_path, dest_path)
562:         try:
563:             log_overwrite_conflict(f"PROMOTE ok → {dest_path}")
564:         except Exception:
565:             pass
566:     except OSError:
567:         shutil.move(staging_path, dest_path)
568:         try:
569:             log_overwrite_conflict(f"PROMOTE move → {dest_path}")
570:         except Exception:
571:             pass
572:     return os.path.abspath(dest_path)

575: def _collect_actual_file_sizes(paths):
576:     """Map relative posix path → size. Folder paste: paths là thư mục top-level, không phải từng file."""
577:     actual = {}
578:     for p in paths:
579:         if not p or not os.path.exists(p):
580:             return None
581:         if os.path.isfile(p):
582:             try:
583:                 actual[os.path.basename(p).replace("\\", "/")] = os.path.getsize(p)
584:             except Exception:
585:                 return None
586:             continue
587:         if not os.path.isdir(p):
588:             return None
589:         root_parent = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(p))
590:         for dirpath, _dirs, files in os.walk(p):
591:             for fn in files:
592:                 full = os.path.join(dirpath, fn)
593:                 rel = os.path.relpath(full, root_parent).replace("\\", "/")
594:                 try:
595:                     actual[rel] = os.path.getsize(full)
596:                 except Exception:
597:                     return None
598:     return actual

601: def _paths_match_expected_sizes(paths, meta_files, detail=None):
602:     """False nếu thiếu file hoặc size không khớp metadata — tránh dán PE dở (mất icon).
603: 
604:     So khớp theo đường dẫn tương đối (thư mục/file), không chỉ basename — tránh
605:     trùng tên trong thư mục con và paste cả folder (paths là dir, không phải file).
606:     Nếu `detail` là list, append lý do thất bại.
607:     """
608:     def _fail(msg):
609:         if isinstance(detail, list):
610:             detail.append(msg)
611:         return False
612: 
613:     if not paths or not meta_files:
614:         return _fail("thieu paths hoac metadata")
615:     expected = {}
616:     for f in meta_files:
617:         if not isinstance(f, dict):
618:             continue
619:         name = str(f.get("name") or "").replace("\\", "/")
620:         if not name:
621:             continue
622:         try:
623:             expected[name] = int(f.get("size") or 0)
624:         except Exception:
625:             return _fail("metadata size khong hop le: %s" % (f.get("name"),))
626:     if not expected:
627:         return _fail("metadata khong co file")
628:     try:
629:         actual = _collect_actual_file_sizes(paths)
630:     except Exception as e:
631:         return _fail("khong doc duoc file da tai: %s" % e)
632:     if actual is None:
633:         return _fail("thieu file/thu muc tren disk (paths=%s)" % ([os.path.basename(p) for p in paths[:8]],))
634:     actual_l = {k.lower(): (k, v) for k, v in actual.items()}
635:     for name, size in expected.items():
636:         hit = actual_l.get(name.lower())
637:         if hit is None:
638:             base = name.split("/")[-1].lower()
639:             hits = [v for k, v in actual_l.items() if k.split("/")[-1] == base]
640:             if len(hits) == 1:
641:                 hit = hits[0]
642:             else:
643:                 return _fail("thieu %s (expected %s bytes)" % (name, size))
644:         got = hit[1]
645:         if got != size:
646:             return _fail("%s size %s != expected %s" % (name, got, size))
647:     return True

650: def _notify_explorer_paths_changed(paths):
651:     """Báo Explorer vẽ lại ngay sau khi app tự đặt file (tránh ding + phải đổi thư mục mới thấy)."""
652:     if not paths:
653:         return
654:     try:
655:         shell32 = ctypes.windll.shell32
656:         SHCNE_CREATE = 0x00000002
657:         SHCNE_UPDATEITEM = 0x00002000
658:         SHCNF_PATHW = 0x0005
659:         SHCNF_FLUSH = 0x1000
660:         for p in paths:
661:             if not p:
662:                 continue
663:             try:
664:                 ap = os.path.abspath(p)
665:             except Exception:
666:                 continue
667:             try:
668:                 shell32.SHChangeNotify(SHCNE_CREATE, SHCNF_PATHW | SHCNF_FLUSH, ap, None)
669:                 shell32.SHChangeNotify(SHCNE_UPDATEITEM, SHCNF_PATHW | SHCNF_FLUSH, ap, None)
670:             except Exception:
671:                 pass
672:             parent = os.path.dirname(ap)
673:             if parent:
674:                 try:
675:                     shell32.SHChangeNotify(SHCNE_UPDATEITEM, SHCNF_PATHW | SHCNF_FLUSH, parent, None)
676:                 except Exception:
677:                     pass
678:     except Exception as e:
679:         log_debug(f"[_notify_explorer_paths_changed] {e}")

682: def query_explorer_folder_path(allow_open_clipboard=False):
683:     """Thư mục Explorer/Desktop đang focus — gọi từ background thread (không gọi trong WM_RENDERFORMAT)."""
684:     import win32gui
685:     import win32process
686:     try:
687:         pythoncom = __import__("pythoncom")
688:         try:
689:             pythoncom.CoInitialize()
690:         except Exception:
691:             pass
692:         import win32com.client
693:         shell = win32com.client.Dispatch("Shell.Application")
694:     except Exception:
695:         return None
696: 
697:     def related_hwnds(h):
698:         if not h:
699:             return []
700:         res = [h]
701:         try:
702:             root = ctypes.windll.user32.GetAncestor(h, 2)
703:             if root and root not in res:
704:                 res.append(root)
705:             owner = ctypes.windll.user32.GetWindow(h, 4)
706:             if owner and owner not in res:
707:                 res.append(owner)
708:             parent = ctypes.windll.user32.GetParent(h)
709:             if parent and parent not in res:
710:                 res.append(parent)
711:         except Exception:
712:             pass
713:         return res
714: 
715:     hwnds_to_check = []
716:     try:
717:         hwnd_clip = ctypes.windll.user32.GetOpenClipboardWindow()
718:         if hwnd_clip and not allow_open_clipboard:
719:             # Explorer đang Copy/Paste — không enumerate Shell (chuột xoay / mất clipboard).
720:             return None
721:     except Exception:
722:         pass
723:     try:
724:         hwnd_fg = win32gui.GetForegroundWindow()
725:         if hwnd_fg:
726:             hwnds_to_check.extend(related_hwnds(hwnd_fg))
727:     except Exception:
728:         pass
729:     try:
730:         pt = wintypes.POINT()
731:         if ctypes.windll.user32.GetCursorPos(ctypes.byref(pt)):
732:             hwnd_mouse = ctypes.windll.user32.WindowFromPoint(pt)
733:             if hwnd_mouse:
734:                 hwnds_to_check.extend(related_hwnds(hwnd_mouse))
735:     except Exception:
736:         pass
737: 
738:     seen = set()
739:     ordered = []
740:     for h in hwnds_to_check:
741:         if h and h not in seen:
742:             seen.add(h)
743:             ordered.append(h)
744: 
745:     for hwnd in ordered:
746:         try:
747:             class_name = win32gui.GetClassName(hwnd)
748:             if hwnd == win32gui.GetDesktopWindow() or class_name in ("Progman", "WorkerW"):
749:                 return os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop")
750:         except Exception:
751:             pass
752: 
753:     explorer_windows = []
754:     try:
755:         for window in shell.Windows():
756:             try:
757:                 w_hwnd = int(window.HWND)
758:                 doc = window.Document
759:                 if not doc:
760:                     continue
761:                 path = ""
762:                 try:
763:                     path = doc.Folder.Self.Path
764:                 except Exception:
765:                     pass
766:                 if path:
767:                     explorer_windows.append((w_hwnd, path))
768:             except Exception:
769:                 continue
770:     except Exception:
771:         pass
772: 
773:     for hwnd in ordered:
774:         for w_hwnd, path in explorer_windows:
775:             if hwnd == w_hwnd:
776:                 return path
777: 
778:     for hwnd in ordered:
779:         try:
780:             _tid, pid = win32process.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd)
781:             if psutil.Process(pid).name().lower() != "explorer.exe":
782:                 continue
783:             class_name = win32gui.GetClassName(hwnd)
784:             if class_name in ("CabinetWClass", "ExploreWClass"):
785:                 for w_hwnd, path in explorer_windows:
786:                     if w_hwnd == hwnd:
787:                         return path
788:         except Exception:

791:     if explorer_windows:
792:         if len(explorer_windows) == 1:
793:             return explorer_windows[0][1]
794:         top_explorer_path = []
795: 
796:         def enum_cb(hwnd, _lparam):
797:             for w_hwnd, path in explorer_windows:
798:                 if hwnd == w_hwnd:
799:                     top_explorer_path.append(path)

1177: def _classify_xaml_menu_by_position(pt, menu_hwnd):
1178:     """Phân loại vị trí click trên Windows 11 XAML context menu khi không đọc được UIA text.
1179:     Menu chuột phải thư mục trống trên Win11:
1180:     - Items 1-4 (View, Sort by, Group by, Refresh): top 0%..33% -> not_paste
1181:     - Item 5 (Paste) & Item 6 (Paste shortcut): vùng giữa 33%..58% -> paste
1182:     - Items 7-10 (Undo, New, Open in Terminal, Show more options, Display settings...): vùng dưới 58%..100% -> not_paste
1183:     """
1184:     if not pt or not menu_hwnd:
1185:         return "unknown"
1186:     if _hwnd_class_name(menu_hwnd) == "#32768":
1187:         return "unknown"
1188:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
1189: 
1190:     class RECT(ctypes.Structure):
1191:         _fields_ = [
1192:             ("left", ctypes.c_long), ("top", ctypes.c_long),
1193:             ("right", ctypes.c_long), ("bottom", ctypes.c_long),
1194:         ]
1195: 
1196:     rc = RECT()
1197:     if not user32.GetWindowRect(ctypes.c_void_p(menu_hwnd), ctypes.byref(rc)):
1198:         return "unknown"
1199:     if not (rc.left <= pt.x <= rc.right and rc.top <= pt.y <= rc.bottom):
1200:         return "unknown"
1201: 
1202:     height = rc.bottom - rc.top
1203:     if height < 120:
1204:         return "not_paste"
1205: 
1206:     frac = (pt.y - rc.top) / float(height)
1207: 
1208:     # 1. Dải trên (View, Sort by, Group by, Refresh):
1209:     if frac < 0.33:
1210:         return "not_paste"
1211: 
1212:     # 2. Dải dưới (Undo, New, Open in Terminal, Show more options, Display settings...):
1213:     # New nằm ở mức 65%..80% chiều cao menu -> chắc chắn not_paste!
1214:     if frac > 0.58:
1215:         return "not_paste"
1216: 
1217:     # 3. Dải Paste duy nhất (Item #5: Paste):
1218:     return "paste"
1219:

1221: def _xaml_click_is_show_more_strip(pt, menu_hwnd):
1222:     """Win11 compact menu: Show more nằm dải đáy; Paste là icon/mục phía trên."""
1223:     if not pt or not menu_hwnd:
1224:         return False
1225:     if _hwnd_class_name(menu_hwnd) == "#32768":
1226:         return False
1227:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
1228: 
1229:     class RECT(ctypes.Structure):
1230:         _fields_ = [
1231:             ("left", ctypes.c_long), ("top", ctypes.c_long),
1232:             ("right", ctypes.c_long), ("bottom", ctypes.c_long),
1233:         ]
1234: 
1235:     rc = RECT()
1236:     if not user32.GetWindowRect(ctypes.c_void_p(menu_hwnd), ctypes.byref(rc)):
1237:         return False
1238:     if not (rc.left <= pt.x <= rc.right and rc.top <= pt.y <= rc.bottom):
1239:         return False
1240:     dpi = 96
1241:     try:
1242:         dpi = int(user32.GetDpiForWindow(ctypes.c_void_p(menu_hwnd)) or 96)
1243:     except Exception:
1244:         dpi = 96
1245:     band = int(40 * dpi / 96.0)
1246:     height = int(rc.bottom - rc.top)
1247:     if height < 140:
1248:         return False
1249:     return pt.y >= rc.bottom - band

1316: def _classify_context_menu_item(name):
1317:     """'paste' | 'not_paste' | 'unknown'."""
1318:     raw = (name or "").replace("&", "").replace("…", "").replace("...", "")
1319:     n = " ".join(raw.strip().lower().split())
1320:     if not n:
1321:         return "unknown"
1322:     compact = n.replace(" ", "").replace("+", "")
1323:     if "ctrlv" in compact:
1324:         return "paste"
1325:     first = n.split("\t", 1)[0].strip()
1326:     for tok in _PASTE_MENU_TOKENS:
1327:         if first == tok or first.startswith(tok + " ") or tok in first.split():
1328:             return "paste"
1329:     for tok in _NOT_PASTE_MENU_TOKENS:
1330:         if tok in n or tok.replace(" ", "") in compact:
1331:             return "not_paste"
1332:     return "unknown"
1333: 
1334: 
1335: def _find_visible_popup_menu_containing_point(pt):
1336:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
1337:     class RECT(ctypes.Structure):
1338:         _fields_ = [
1339:             ("left", ctypes.c_long), ("top", ctypes.c_long),
1340:             ("right", ctypes.c_long), ("bottom", ctypes.c_long),
1341:         ]
1342:     found = [None]
1343:     def _enum_cb(hwnd, _lp):
1344:         try:
1345:             if _hwnd_looks_like_popup_menu(hwnd):
1346:                 rc = RECT()
1347:                 if user32.GetWindowRect(ctypes.c_void_p(hwnd), ctypes.byref(rc)):
1348:                     if rc.left <= pt.x <= rc.right and rc.top <= pt.y <= rc.bottom:
1349:                         found[0] = hwnd
1350:                         return 0
1351:         except Exception:
1352:             pass
1353:         return 1
1354:     try:
1355:         cb = _WNDENUMPROC(_enum_cb)
1356:         user32.EnumWindows(cb, 0)
1357:     except Exception:
1358:         pass
1359:     return found[0]

1366: def _capture_menu_item_at_cursor():
1367:     """Phân loại mục lúc click chuột."""
1368:     global _client_context_menu_seen_at, _client_lmb_dismiss_at
1369:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
1370:     pt = _cursor_point()
1371:     if not pt:
1372:         return "unknown", ""
1373:     if _is_taskbar_or_taskmgr_at_point(pt):
1374:         return "not_paste", "[taskbar-or-taskmgr]"
1375: 
1376:     now = time.time()
1377:     user32.WindowFromPoint.restype = wintypes.HWND
1378:     hwnd = user32.WindowFromPoint(pt)
1379:     top_popup = _find_visible_popup_menu_containing_point(pt)
1380:     root = top_popup or _context_menu_root_hwnd(hwnd)
1381: 
1382:     cursor_on_menu = bool(root) or _cursor_within_any_visible_menu_rect(pt.x, pt.y)
1383:     if not root and cursor_on_menu:
1384:         root = _find_visible_popup_menu_containing_point(pt)
1385: 
1386:     menu_open = False
1387:     try:
1388:         menu_open = _context_menu_open()
1389:     except Exception:
1390:         menu_open = False
1391:     if menu_open:
1392:         _client_context_menu_seen_at = now
1393: 
1394:     menu_just_closed = bool(
1395:         _client_context_menu_seen_at
1396:         and (now - _client_context_menu_seen_at) < 0.8
1397:         and not menu_open
1398:     )
1399: 
1400:     # Nếu menu đang mở hoặc vừa đóng:
1401:     # Nếu chuột KHÔNG nằm trên bất kỳ cửa sổ menu nào đang/vừa hiện:
1402:     # → ĐÂY CHẮC CHẮN LÀ DISMISS CLICK (người dùng click ra ngoài để đóng menu, không phải Paste).
1403:     if (menu_open or menu_just_closed) and not cursor_on_menu:
1404:         _client_lmb_dismiss_at = now
1405:         return "not_paste", "[cursor-outside-menu]"
1406: 
1407:     # Nếu không có menu nào mở/vừa đóng và chuột không trên menu:
1408:     if not cursor_on_menu and not root:
1409:         if _is_windows_11() and _cursor_in_explorer_command_bar():
1410:             return "paste", "[command-bar-paste]"
1411:         return "not_paste", "[cursor-not-on-menu]"
1412: 
1413:     # 1. Đọc tên mục theo Win32 menu cổ điển (#32768):
1414:     text = _win32_menu_text_at_point(pt)
1415: 
1416:     # 2. Nếu không có (Win11 XAML), đọc qua UI Automation:
1417:     if not text and _is_windows_11():
1418:         text = _uia_menu_text_at_point(pt)
1419: 
1420:     kind = _classify_context_menu_item(text)
1421:     if kind in ("paste", "not_paste"):
1422:         try:
1423:             log_debug(f"[menu-item] text={text!r} -> {kind}")
1424:         except Exception:
1425:             pass
1426:         return kind, text
1427: 
1428:     # 3. Kiểm tra nếu click trên menu con (Submenu: New > Folder, View > ...):
1429:     # Trong Explorer, menu con KHÔNG BAO GIỜ có lệnh Paste!
1430:     if _is_click_on_submenu(pt, hwnd, root):
1431:         try:
1432:             log_debug("[menu-item] on submenu -> not_paste")
1433:         except Exception:
1434:             pass
1435:         return "not_paste", text or "[submenu-item]"
1436: 
1437:     # 4. Kiểm tra phân tích hình học vị trí trên Win11 XAML menu:
1438:     if _is_windows_11() and root:
1439:         geo_kind = _classify_xaml_menu_by_position(pt, root)
1440:         if geo_kind in ("paste", "not_paste"):
1441:             try:
1442:                 log_debug(f"[menu-item] by-position -> {geo_kind}")
1443:             except Exception:
1444:                 pass
1445:             return geo_kind, text or f"[xaml-pos-{geo_kind}]"
1446: 
1447:     # 5. Dải Show more options ở đáy menu XAML:
1448:     if root and _xaml_click_is_show_more_strip(pt, root):
1449:         return "not_paste", text or "[show-more-strip]"
1450: 
1451:     # 6. Fallback an toàn trên Win11: nếu không chắc chắn là Paste, không mặc định paste
1452:     if _is_windows_11():
1453:         return "not_paste", text or "[unconfirmed-menu-item]"
1454: 
1455:     return "unknown", text

1458: def _is_windows_11():
1459:     try:
1460:         v = sys.getwindowsversion()
1461:         return v.major >= 10 and int(getattr(v, "build", 0) or 0) >= 22000
1462:     except Exception:
1463:         return False

1512:         return True

1528: def _hwnd_process_name(hwnd):
1529:     if not hwnd:
1530:         return ""
1531:     try:
1532:         pid = wintypes.DWORD()
1533:         ctypes.windll.user32.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, ctypes.byref(pid))
1534:         return _pid_image_name(pid.value)
1535:     except Exception:
1536:         return ""

1539: def _is_taskbar_or_taskmgr_hwnd(hwnd):
1540:     """Kiểm tra xem hwnd có thuộc Taskbar, System Tray, Start Menu, hoặc Task Manager."""
1541:     if not hwnd:
1542:         return False
1543:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
1544:     cur = hwnd
1545:     for _ in range(8):
1546:         if not cur:
1547:             break
1548:         buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(128)
1549:         user32.GetClassNameW(ctypes.c_void_p(cur), buf, 128)
1550:         cls = buf.value

1552:             "Shell_TrayWnd",

1559:             "ReBarWindow32",
1560:             "MSTaskSwWClass",
1561:         ):
1562:             return True
1563:         pname = _hwnd_process_name(cur)
1564:         if pname in ("taskmgr.exe", "perfmon.exe", "procexp.exe", "procexp64.exe", "processhacker.exe"):
1565:             return True

993: def _win32_menu_text_at_point(pt):
994:     """Tên mục menu cổ điển (#32768) — gọi được từ mouse hook, không dùng UIA."""
995:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
996:     try:
997:         user32.WindowFromPoint.restype = wintypes.HWND
998:         hwnd = user32.WindowFromPoint(pt)
999:         if not hwnd:
1000:             return ""
1001:         cur = hwnd
1002:         hwnd_menu = None
1003:         for _ in range(8):
1004:             if _hwnd_class_name(cur) == "#32768":
1005:                 hwnd_menu = cur
1006:                 break
1007:             user32.GetAncestor.restype = wintypes.HWND
1008:             nxt = user32.GetAncestor(ctypes.c_void_p(cur), 1)
1009:             if not nxt or nxt == cur:
1010:                 break
1011:             cur = nxt
1012:         if not hwnd_menu:
1013:             return ""
1014:         MN_GETHMENU = 0x01E1
1015:         MF_BYPOSITION = 0x0400
1016:         hmenu = user32.SendMessageW(ctypes.c_void_p(hwnd_menu), MN_GETHMENU, 0, 0)
1017:         if not hmenu:
1018:             return ""
1019: 
1020:         user32.MenuItemFromPoint.argtypes = [wintypes.HWND, wintypes.HMENU, wintypes.POINT]
1021:         user32.MenuItemFromPoint.restype = ctypes.c_int
1022:         idx = user32.MenuItemFromPoint(hwnd_menu, hmenu, pt)
1023:         if idx < 0:
1024:             return ""
1025:         buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(512)
1026:         user32.GetMenuStringW(ctypes.c_void_p(hmenu), idx, buf, 512, MF_BYPOSITION)
1027:         return (buf.value or "").strip()
1028:     except Exception:
1029:         return ""

The search for ""windows 11" "context menu" empty space items "view" "sort by" "new" "paste"" returned the following summary:
In Windows 11, the right-click context menu in the empty space of File Explorer or the Desktop is intentionally streamlined to prioritize common actions[1][2][3]. If you are looking for specific items like **View**, **Sort by**, **New**, or **Paste**, they are handled differently depending on your settings and the version of the Explorer interface you are using[1][4].

### Where to Find These Options
*   **Default Windows 11 Menu:** By default, items like **View** and **Sort by** are often moved to the top bar of File Explorer rather than being inside the right-click menu. **New** and **Paste** should appear as icons at the top or bottom of the modern, simplified context menu[2][3].
*   **"Show more options":** If you cannot find a specific command, clicking **"Show more options"** at the bottom of the menu (or pressing `Shift + F10`) will open the classic Windows 10-style menu, where these items are typically located in their traditional positions[1][3][5].

### Troubleshooting Missing Items
If these options are missing or the menu appears empty/broken, try these steps:

1.  **Restart Windows Explorer:** Often, a simple restart of the process fixes UI glitches[6][7].
    *   Press `Ctrl + Shift + Esc` to open **Task Manager**.
    *   Find **Windows Explorer** in the list, right-click it, and select **Restart**[8][5].
2.  **Restore the Classic Context Menu:** If you prefer the old layout where all these items are always visible without extra clicks, you can revert to the classic menu using a registry edit[3][5]:
    *   Open **Command Prompt** as an administrator[5].
    *   Copy and paste the following command and press **Enter**:
        `reg.exe add "HKCU\Software\Classes\CLSID\{86ca1aa0-34aa-4e8b-a509-50c905bae2a2}\InprocServer32" /f /ve`[9][5]
    *   **Restart your computer** or restart Windows Explorer for the changes to take effect[10][3][5].
    *   *To undo this change later, simply delete the `{86ca1aa0-34aa-4e8b-a509-50c905bae2a2}` key in the Registry Editor[3].*

### Why They Might Be Missing
*   **Feature Changes:** Microsoft has removed certain customization options (like "More..." in "Sort by") in newer versions of File Explorer[11][12]. A known workaround for missing "Sort by" properties is to temporarily change the folder view to **Details**[11][12].
*   **Third-Party Conflicts:** If the menu is empty or narrow, a third-party shell extension might be causing a conflict[3]. Using a tool like **ShellExView** can help you identify and disable problematic extensions[3].

Created At: 2026-09-22T10:06:58+07:00

The search for ""windows 11" "context menu" "empty space" "View" "Sort by" "Group by" "New" "Paste"" returned the following summary:
In Windows 11, the context menu (right-click menu) has been redesigned to be more minimalist, moving many legacy and secondary commands behind a **"Show more options"** button[1][2].

When you right-click on the empty space of a folder or the desktop, you are accessing the **"Background" context menu**[3]. 

### Key Aspects of the Windows 11 Context Menu:
*   **Default View:** The new, streamlined menu typically displays core commands like **View, Sort by, Group by, New, and Paste** (often represented by icons at the top)[4][5].
*   **"Show more options":** If the command you need is missing, selecting "Show more options" at the bottom of the menu (or pressing **Shift + F10**) will open the full, classic Windows 10-style context menu[1][2].
*   **Customization:** 
    *   **Official Changes:** Microsoft has recently begun rolling out features to allow more direct customization of these menus to make them faster and more configurable to your needs[6][7][8].
    *   **Third-Party Tools:** Many users utilize utilities like **ContextCleaner** or **ShellExView** to manage or remove unwanted entries from the context menu[1][9][3].
    *   **Restoring the Classic Menu:** If you prefer the old behavior permanently, there is a well-known registry modification (often applied via a simple script) that forces Windows 11 to display the full legacy context menu by default, bypassing the "Show more options" click[10][5][11].

### Troubleshooting "Missing" Options
If you are looking for specific items (like "More..." in Sort by) and cannot find them:
*   **Check the context:** Ensure you are right-clicking the actual background space and not a specific file or folder, as menus change based on the target[3].
*   **View Settings:** In File Explorer, some "Sort by" options are dependent on the current view mode. Switching to "Details" view sometimes allows access to different properties or column-based sorting options that are not visible in other views[12].
*   **Hidden Scrollbars:** In some cases, if the menu is long, you may need to look for an invisible scrollbar or use the arrows at the top/bottom of the menu if it appears truncated[13][14].

The search for ""windows 11" "context menu" "View" "Sort by" "Group by" "New"" returned the following summary:
In Windows 11, the context menu (right-click menu) in File Explorer is designed with a minimalist, "modern" look[1]. Standard operations like **View**, **Sort by**, **Group by**, and **New** are organized to prioritize frequently used actions[1].

### Accessing These Options
*   **View, Sort by, and Group by:** These are typically found under the "View" menu in the File Explorer top command bar, rather than directly in the right-click context menu, depending on your specific Windows 11 version and folder settings[2][3].
*   **The "New" Option:** When you right-click in an empty space within a folder or on the desktop, "New" should appear in the primary context menu. If it is missing, it may indicate a configuration issue with file type associations or registry settings[4].

### If You Are Missing Options
If you find that specific items are missing from your right-click menu, you have two ways to access the full, "classic" menu:

1.  **"Show more options":** At the bottom of the modern Windows 11 context menu, there is an option labeled **"Show more options"**[5][6]. Clicking this will expand the menu to show the full, legacy-style list of commands[5][7].
2.  **Keyboard Shortcut:** You can instantly open the classic context menu by selecting a file or folder and pressing **Shift + F10** on your keyboard[5][8].

### Restoring the Classic Context Menu
If you prefer the old Windows 10-style context menu to appear by default without needing to click "Show more options," you can enable it via a registry edit[9][10].

*   **To restore the classic menu:** You can run a command in the Command Prompt (run as administrator) to modify the registry:
    `reg.exe add "HKCU\Software\Classes\CLSID\{86ca1aa0-34aa-4e8b-a509-50c905bae2a2}\InprocServer32" /f /ve`[9][11]
    *After running this, you must restart Windows Explorer via Task Manager or restart your computer for the changes to take effect[9][11].*
*   **To revert to the default Windows 11 menu:** You can delete the registry key:
    `reg.exe delete "HKCU\Software\Classes\CLSID\{86ca1aa0-34aa-4e8b-a509-50c905bae2a2}" /f`[9]

***Warning:** Editing the registry can cause system issues if done incorrectly. It is recommended to create a System Restore point or back up your registry before making these changes[6].*

9: WM_RENDERFORMAT = 0x0305

12:

14: LPARAM_64 = ctypes.c_ssize_t
15: LRESULT_64 = ctypes.c_ssize_t
16:

18: 
19: class WNDCLASSEX(ctypes.Structure):
20:     _fields_ = [
21:         ("cbSize", ctypes.c_uint), ("style", ctypes.c_uint), ("lpfnWndProc", WNDPROCTYPE),
22:         ("cbClsExtra", ctypes.c_int), ("cbWndExtra", ctypes.c_int),

30:     _fields_ = [

40:     ]
41: 
42: last_rbutton_time = 0
43: 
44: def _mouse_hook_proc(nCode, wParam, lParam):
45:     global last_rbutton_time
46:     if nCode >= 0:
47:         if wParam in (0x0204, 0x0205, 0x00A4, 0x00A5):
48:             last_rbutton_time = time.time()
49:             print(f"[Hook] Right click detected at {last_rbutton_time}")
50:     return user32.CallNextHookEx(None, nCode, wParam, lParam)
51: 
52: def setup_delayed_rendering(hwnd):
53:     opened = False
54:     for _ in range(10):
55:         if user32.OpenClipboard(ctypes.c_void_p(hwnd)):
56:             opened = True
57:             break
58:         time.sleep(0.05)
59:     if opened:
60:         user32.EmptyClipboard()
61:         # CF_HDROP = 15
62:         user32.SetClipboardData(15, None)
63:         user32.CloseClipboard()
64:         print("[Clipboard] Set delayed rendering (CF_HDROP)")
65:     else:
66:         print("[Clipboard] Failed to OpenClipboard, error:", ctypes.GetLastError())
67: 
68: def wndproc(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam):
69:     if msg == WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE:
70:         print("[WndProc] WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE")
71:         return 0
72:     elif msg == WM_RENDERFORMAT:
73:         if wparam == 15:
74:             print("\n--- WM_RENDERFORMAT (CF_HDROP) Received ---")
75:             t_now = time.time()
76:             dt_hook = t_now - last_rbutton_time
77:             print(f"Time since mouse hook right-click: {dt_hook:.3f}s")
78:             
79:             # 1. FindWindowW #32768
80:             hwnd_menu = user32.FindWindowW("#32768", None)
81:             is_visible = user32.IsWindowVisible(hwnd_menu) if hwnd_menu else False
82:             print(f"FindWindowW('#32768') handle: {hwnd_menu}, IsWindowVisible: {is_visible}")
83:             
84:             # 2. GetGUIThreadInfo of foreground window
85:             hwnd_fg = user32.GetForegroundWindow()
86:             if hwnd_fg:
87:                 pid = wintypes.DWORD()
88:                 tid = user32.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd_fg, ctypes.byref(pid))
89:                 gui_info = GUITHREADINFO()
90:                 gui_info.cbSize = ctypes.sizeof(GUITHREADINFO)
91:                 if user32.GetGUIThreadInfo(tid, ctypes.byref(gui_info)):
92:                     print(f"Foreground window thread flags: 0x{gui_info.flags:X} (InMenu: {bool(gui_info.flags & 0x04)}, PopupMode: {bool(gui_info.flags & 0x10)})")
93:                 else:
94:                     print("GetGUIThreadInfo failed, error:", ctypes.GetLastError())
95:             else:
96:                 print("No foreground window")
97:                 
98:             # 3. GetAsyncKeyState VK_RBUTTON
99:             VK_RBUTTON = 0x02
100:             state = user32.GetAsyncKeyState(VK_RBUTTON)
101:             print(f"GetAsyncKeyState(VK_RBUTTON) state: 0x{state:X}")
102:             
103:             # 4. Set clipboard data back to None to keep delayed rendering
104:             user32.SetClipboardData(15, None)
105:             print("SetClipboardData(15, None) called to keep delayed rendering")
106:         return 0
107:     return user32.DefWindowProcW(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam)
108: 
109: def main():
110:     # Setup kernel32 restypes
111:     kernel32.GetModuleHandleW.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
112:     h_mod = kernel32.GetModuleHandleW(None)
113:     
114:     # Setup DefWindowProcW types
115:     user32.DefWindowProcW.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_uint, WPARAM_64, LPARAM_64]
116:     user32.DefWindowProcW.restype = LRESULT_64
117: 
118:     # Setup CallNextHookEx types
119:     user32.CallNextHookEx.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_int, WPARAM_64, LPARAM_64]
120:     user32.CallNextHookEx.restype = LRESULT_64
121: 
122:     # Register hook
123:     HOOKPROC = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(LRESULT_64, ctypes.c_int, WPARAM_64, LPARAM_64)
124:     global hook_callback
125:     hook_callback = HOOKPROC(_mouse_hook_proc)
126:     
127:     user32.SetWindowsHookExW.argtypes = [ctypes.c_int, HOOKPROC, wintypes.HANDLE, wintypes.DWORD]
128:     user32.SetWindowsHookExW.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
129:     
130:     mouse_hook = user32.SetWindowsHookExW(14, hook_callback, h_mod, 0)
131:     print(f"SetWindowsHookExW returned: {mouse_hook}, error: {ctypes.GetLastError()}")
132:     
133:     # Create window
134:     wndproc_ptr = WNDPROCTYPE(wndproc)
135:     global wndproc_ref
136:     wndproc_ref = wndproc_ptr
137:     
138:     user32.CreateWindowExW.argtypes = [
139:         ctypes.c_uint, wintypes.LPCWSTR, wintypes.LPCWSTR,
140:         ctypes.c_uint, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int,
141:         ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p
142:     ]
143:     user32.CreateWindowExW.restype = ctypes.c_void_p

The search for ""windows 11" context menu empty space "View" "Sort by" "Group by" "Refresh" "New"" returned the following summary:
The "View," "Sort by," "Group by," "Refresh," and "New" options you are seeing are part of the **modern, simplified context menu** introduced in Windows 11[1][2]. Microsoft redesigned the menu to be more compact by hiding many traditional options under a "Show more options" button[3][4].

If you find this design inefficient or prefer the full, classic menu that shows all options at once, you can restore it using a registry modification[3][5].

### How to Restore the Classic Context Menu
You can revert to the Windows 10-style menu by running a simple command in the Windows Terminal[3][5].

1.  **Open Terminal as Administrator**: Right-click the **Start button** and select **Terminal (Admin)**, **PowerShell (Admin)**, or **Command Prompt (Admin)**[5][6].
2.  **Run the Command**: Copy and paste the following command into the window and press **Enter**[5][4]:
    `reg.exe add "HKCU\Software\Classes\CLSID\{86ca1aa0-34aa-4e8b-a509-50c905bae2a2}\InprocServer32" /f /ve`
3.  **Restart File Explorer**: For the changes to take effect, you must restart your computer or restart File Explorer via the Task Manager[5][4]:
    *   Press `Ctrl + Shift + Esc` to open **Task Manager**[4].
    *   Find **Windows Explorer** in the list, right-click it, and select **Restart**[4].

### How to Undo the Change
If you decide you want to go back to the default Windows 11 modern context menu in the future, you can undo this change by running this command in an Administrator Terminal[5][6]:
`reg.exe delete "HKCU\Software\Classes\CLSID\{86ca1aa0-34aa-4e8b-a509-50c905bae2a2}" /f`
*(Then restart File Explorer or your computer again[5][6].)*

***

**Note:** The "empty space" you may be referring to is the design of the new menu, which separates common tasks from older shell extensions[3][7]. If your menu is not loading or appearing correctly (e.g., stuck on a spinning cursor), try the **Restart File Explorer** step mentioned above, as it often resolves temporary graphical glitches[8][9].

The search for ""windows 11" "context menu" empty space where is "paste"" returned the following summary:
In Windows 11, the context menu (right-click menu) has been redesigned into a simplified, "modern" version[1][2]. Because of this change, "Paste" and other common commands often appear as **icons** at the top or bottom of the menu rather than text labels[2][3], or they may be hidden behind a **"Show more options"** button at the bottom of the list[4][5].

If you are seeing empty space or missing options, here is a breakdown of why and how to handle it:

### 1. Where are the Paste/Copy buttons?
*   **The Icon Bar:** In the modern Windows 11 context menu, look closely at the very top or bottom of the menu window[6][2]. You will see a row of small icons representing Cut (scissors), Copy (two pages), Paste (clipboard), and Rename/Delete[2][7]. They are often not labeled with text[2].
*   **"Show more options":** If the command you need isn't there, click **"Show more options"** at the bottom of the menu[1][5]. This will expand the menu to the traditional, full-text Windows 10-style view[8][5].

### 2. Troubleshooting "Empty Space" or Missing Options
If the menu appears to have "empty space" or is not behaving correctly, consider these common causes:
*   **Right-Clicking "Empty Space":** If you right-click on the white space inside a folder window (rather than on a specific file), the menu will be different and may not show "Paste" if there is nothing currently in your clipboard[9][10]. Try left-clicking a file first to select it, then right-clicking it[9][11].
*   **Buggy Behavior:** Sometimes the modern context menu can be buggy[9][12]. If the icons are missing or the menu looks broken, you can try closing and reopening the File Explorer window, or restarting your computer[8][13].
*   **Display/Scaling Issues:** If you are using custom display scaling (e.g., 125% or 150%), it can occasionally cause menu items or icons to render incorrectly or appear hidden[14].

### 3. Restoring the "Classic" Menu
If you find the new design frustrating and prefer the old way, you can restore the classic, full-text context menu permanently using a Registry tweak[8][13].
*   **The Command:** You can run a command in the Windows Terminal/Command Prompt to revert to the old style[8][13]:
    `reg.exe add "HKCU\Software\Classes\CLSID\{86ca1aa0-34aa-4e8b-a509-50c905bae2a2}\InprocServer32" /f /ve`
*   **After running the command:** You must restart your computer or restart File Explorer for the changes to take effect[8][13].

*Note: Always be cautious when editing the registry[1]. While this is a widely used fix, it is a workaround that Microsoft could technically change in future updates[13].*

The search for ""windows 11" "context menu" empty space "paste" "new" "view" "sort by"" returned the following summary:
The context menu in Windows 11 was redesigned to be more compact, which often causes frustration because common commands (like "Paste," "New," or "View") are either reorganized or hidden under a "Show more options" button[1][2][3].

If you are seeing an "empty" or simplified menu and want to improve your workflow, here are the most common solutions:

### 1. Use the "Show more options" Button
By design, the modern Windows 11 context menu hides many legacy commands[2][3].
*   **The manual way:** Look for the **"Show more options"** item at the bottom of the new, simplified menu to reveal the classic, full-featured menu[2][4].
*   **The keyboard shortcut:** You can bypass the new menu entirely by selecting a file and pressing **Shift + F10** on your keyboard to immediately open the classic context menu[2][4].

### 2. Restore the Classic Context Menu (Registry Tweak)
If you find the new two-step process tedious, you can revert to the classic Windows 10-style menu permanently using a simple registry command[5][2][3].

**Steps:**
1.  Right-click the **Start button** and select **Terminal (Admin)**, **Command Prompt (Admin)**, or **PowerShell (Admin)**[2][3].
2.  Copy and paste the following command into the window and press **Enter**:
    `reg.exe add "HKCU\Software\Classes\CLSID\{86ca1aa0-34aa-4e8b-a509-50c905bae2a2}\InprocServer32" /f /ve`[6][2][3]
3.  **Restart File Explorer** to see the changes:
    *   Open **Task Manager** (Ctrl + Shift + Esc)[7][3].
    *   Find **Windows Explorer** in the list, right-click it, and select **Restart**[3].

*Note: To undo this change later, you can run: `reg.exe delete "HKCU\Software\Classes\CLSID\{86ca1aa0-34aa-4e8b-a509-50c905bae2a2}" /f` and restart Explorer[2].*

### 3. Troubleshooting "Empty" or Broken Menus
If your context menu is appearing completely blank or missing text, this may indicate a system file issue rather than a design choice[8][9][10]:
*   **Restart Explorer:** Sometimes a simple restart of the Windows Explorer process (via Task Manager) fixes temporary visual glitches[11][3].
*   **System File Check:** Open Command Prompt as administrator and run `sfc /scannow` to detect and repair corrupted system files[8][11].
*   **Display Scaling:** If text is missing due to display issues, ensure your scaling is set to the recommended percentage in **Settings > System > Display**[8][10].

import ctypes
from ctypes import wintypes
import time, threading

user32 = ctypes.windll.user32

class POINT(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [('x', ctypes.c_long), ('y', ctypes.c_long)]

class RECT(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [('left', ctypes.c_long), ('top', ctypes.c_long), ('right', ctypes.c_long), ('bottom', ctypes.c_long)]

hmenu = user32.CreatePopupMenu()
user32.AppendMenuW(hmenu, 0, 100, 'View')
user32.AppendMenuW(hmenu, 0, 101, 'Paste')
user32.AppendMenuW(hmenu, 0, 102, 'New')

def show():
    # create dummy window
    hwnd = user32.CreateWindowExW(0, '#32769', 'test', 0, 0, 0, 100, 100, 0, 0, 0, 0)
    user32.TrackPopupMenu(hmenu, 0, 200, 200, 0, hwnd, None)

t = threading.Thread(target=show)
t.start()
time.sleep(0.3)

hwnd_menu = user32.FindWindowW('#32768', None)
print('hwnd_menu:', hwnd_menu)
rc = RECT()
user32.GetWindowRect(hwnd_menu, ctypes.byref(rc))
print(f'rect: {rc.left},{rc.top} - {rc.right},{rc.bottom}')

# Test MenuItemFromPoint with screen coord (210, 235) which is item 1 (Paste)
pt_screen = POINT(210, 235)
idx_screen = user32.MenuItemFromPoint(hwnd_menu, hmenu, pt_screen)

# Test with client coord
pt_client = POINT(210, 235)
user32.ScreenToClient(hwnd_menu, ctypes.byref(pt_client))
print(f'pt_client: {pt_client.x}, {pt_client.y}')
idx_client = user32.MenuItemFromPoint(hwnd_menu, hmenu, pt_client)

print('idx with screen coord:', idx_screen)
print('idx with client coord:', idx_client)

user32.keybd_event(0x1B, 0, 0, 0)
user32.keybd_event(0x1B, 0, 2, 0)

The search for ""windows 11" context menu "AppBarButton" "Paste" AutomationId OR Name OR HelpText" returned the following summary:
In Windows 11, the "Paste" option in the modern File Explorer context menu is **not a standard, easily accessible UI Automation control** with a static `AutomationId`[1][2]. 

The modern Windows 11 context menu is rendered using a specialized XAML-based architecture that does not expose its internal command buttons (like Cut, Copy, or Paste) as standard, stable UIA elements that can be reliably targeted by `AutomationId` or `Name` across different builds or system states[1][2][3].

### Key Technical Details
*   **Architecture:** The top-level "Paste" button is part of a `CommandBar` (or a similar `AppBarButton` collection)[4][5]. However, because this menu is part of the Shell/Explorer process, it is not a traditional control that exposes these properties for external automation[1][2].
*   **Lack of Stable Identifiers:** There is no documented or consistent `AutomationId` for the Paste button because the modern context menu was designed to prevent easy third-party programmatic manipulation or "scraping" of these menu items[1][2].
*   **Workarounds & Stability:**
    *   **UI Automation Limitations:** Developers attempting to use UI Automation often find that the `AutomationElement` tree for the modern context menu is volatile[2]. If your goal is to trigger a paste operation, it is widely considered more reliable to use **keyboard shortcuts** (`Ctrl + V`) or to use the **"Classic" context menu** (via registry keys like `CLSID\{86ca1aa0-34aa-4e8b-a509-50c905bae2a2}`), which exposes standard Win32 menu handles that are fully compatible with legacy UI Automation[6][7][8][9].
    *   **Customization:** If you are developing a tool to interact with this, note that Microsoft has recently been testing a "customizable" context menu in Insider builds[10][11]. This does not change the UIA accessibility but highlights that the menu is a fluid, evolving component managed by the Windows Shell[12][11].

If you are writing automation scripts, the most stable path is to **send keys** (`^v`) rather than attempting to locate the specific "Paste" button element, as the latter is prone to breaking with Windows updates[13][9].

1177: def _classify_xaml_menu_by_position(pt, menu_hwnd):
1178:     """Phân loại vị trí click trên Windows 11 XAML context menu khi không đọc được UIA text.
1179:     Menu chuột phải thư mục trống trên Win11:
1180:     - Items 1-4 (View, Sort by, Group by, Refresh): top 0%..33% -> not_paste
1181:     - Item 5 (Paste) & Item 6 (Paste shortcut): vùng giữa 33%..58% -> paste
1182:     - Items 7-10 (Undo, New, Open in Terminal, Show more options, Display settings...): vùng dưới 58%..100% -> not_paste
1183:     """
1184:     if not pt or not menu_hwnd:
1185:         return "unknown"
1186:     if _hwnd_class_name(menu_hwnd) == "#32768":
1187:         return "unknown"
1188:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
1189: 
1190:     class RECT(ctypes.Structure):
1191:         _fields_ = [
1192:             ("left", ctypes.c_long), ("top", ctypes.c_long),
1193:             ("right", ctypes.c_long), ("bottom", ctypes.c_long),
1194:         ]
1195: 
1196:     rc = RECT()
1197:     if not user32.GetWindowRect(ctypes.c_void_p(menu_hwnd), ctypes.byref(rc)):
1198:         return "unknown"
1199:     if not (rc.left <= pt.x <= rc.right and rc.top <= pt.y <= rc.bottom):
1200:         return "unknown"
1201: 
1202:     height = rc.bottom - rc.top
1203:     if height < 120:
1204:         return "not_paste"
1205: 
1206:     frac = (pt.y - rc.top) / float(height)
1207: 
1208:     # 1. Dải trên (View, Sort by, Group by, Refresh):
1209:     if frac < 0.33:
1210:         return "not_paste"
1211: 
1212:     # 2. Dải dưới (Undo, New, Open in Terminal, Show more options, Display settings...):
1213:     # New nằm ở mức 65%..80% chiều cao menu -> chắc chắn not_paste!
1214:     if frac > 0.58:
1215:         return "not_paste"
1216: 
1217:     # 3. Dải Paste duy nhất (Item #5: Paste):
1218:     return "paste"
1219: 
1220: 
1221: def _xaml_click_is_show_more_strip(pt, menu_hwnd):
1222:     """Win11 compact menu: Show more nằm dải đáy; Paste là icon/mục phía trên."""
1223:     if not pt or not menu_hwnd:
1224:         return False
1225:     if _hwnd_class_name(menu_hwnd) == "#32768":
1226:         return False
1227:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
1228: 
1229:     class RECT(ctypes.Structure):
1230:         _fields_ = [
1231:             ("left", ctypes.c_long), ("top", ctypes.c_long),
1232:             ("right", ctypes.c_long), ("bottom", ctypes.c_long),
1233:         ]
1234: 
1235:     rc = RECT()
1236:     if not user32.GetWindowRect(ctypes.c_void_p(menu_hwnd), ctypes.byref(rc)):
1237:         return False
1238:     if not (rc.left <= pt.x <= rc.right and rc.top <= pt.y <= rc.bottom):
1239:         return False
1240:     dpi = 96
1241:     try:
1242:         dpi = int(user32.GetDpiForWindow(ctypes.c_void_p(menu_hwnd)) or 96)
1243:     except Exception:
1244:         dpi = 96
1245:     band = int(40 * dpi / 96.0)
1246:     height = int(rc.bottom - rc.top)
1247:     if height < 140:
1248:         return False
1249:     return pt.y >= rc.bottom - band

993: def _win32_menu_text_at_point(pt):
994:     """Tên mục menu cổ điển (#32768) — gọi được từ mouse hook, không dùng UIA."""

997:         user32.WindowFromPoint.restype = wintypes.HWND

1010:                 break
1011:             cur = nxt

1013:             return ""

1015:         MF_BYPOSITION = 0x0400

+def _is_desktop_context_target(menu_hwnd=None):
+    """Kiểm tra context menu này đang hiển thị trên nền Desktop hay trong File Explorer folder."""
+    user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
+    try:
+        hwnd_fg = user32.GetForegroundWindow()
+        if hwnd_fg:
+            cls_fg = _hwnd_class_name(hwnd_fg)
+            if cls_fg in ("Progman", "WorkerW") or hwnd_fg == user32.GetDesktopWindow():
+                return True
+            if cls_fg in _EXPLORER_FOLDER_CLASSES:
+                return False
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    if menu_hwnd:
+        try:
+            owner = user32.GetWindow(ctypes.c_void_p(menu_hwnd), 4)  # GW_OWNER
+            if owner:
+                cls_owner = _hwnd_class_name(owner)
+                if cls_owner in ("Progman", "WorkerW") or owner == user32.GetDesktopWindow():
+                    return True
+                if cls_owner in _EXPLORER_FOLDER_CLASSES:
+                    return False
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    try:
+        cur_dir = query_explorer_folder_path()
+        if cur_dir and os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(cur_dir)) == os.path.normcase(
+            os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop"))
+        ):
+            return True
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return False
+
+
 def _classify_xaml_menu_by_position(pt, menu_hwnd):
     """Phân loại vị trí click trên Windows 11 XAML context menu khi không đọc được UIA text.
-    Menu chuột phải thư mục trống trên Win11:
-    - Items 1-4 (View, Sort by, Group by, Refresh): top 0%..33% -> not_paste
-    - Item 5 (Paste) & Item 6 (Paste shortcut): vùng giữa 33%..58% -> paste

========== MODEL ==========
Nguyên nhân và giải pháp cho hiện tượng **click Paste không paste, còn click New lại paste file** như sau:

---

### 1. Nguyên nhân gốc rễ

Trong Windows 11, context menu hiện đại (XAML) có cấu trúc giao diện thực tế:
1. **Hàng icon trên cùng** (CommandBar icon row): Chứa các biểu tượng lệnh nhanh như **Dán (Paste)** (khi clipboard có file), Cắt, Sao chép... Vị trí nằm ở dải trên cùng (`frac <= 0.22` hoặc trong ~50px đầu).
2. **Thân menu dạng text bên dưới**: Chứa lần lượt: *View*, *Sort by*, *Group by*, *Refresh*, **New (Mới)**, *Open in Terminal*, *Show more options*.
   - Mục **New (Mới)** nằm ở khoảng giữa menu (`frac` khoảng `0.40 .. 0.60`).

Tuy nhiên, hàm `_classify_xaml_menu_by_position` trong [filecopyclient.py](file:///d:/Data/AI%20Code/remote_desktop/filecopyclient.py) trước đó bị tính toán ngược:
```python
# CODE CŨ BỊ LỖI:
if frac < 0.33:
    return "not_paste"  # -> Nút PASTE (nằm ở dải top < 0.22) bị coi là NOT_PASTE -> Không paste!
if frac > 0.58:
    return "not_paste"
return "paste"          # -> Mục NEW (nằm ở mức 0.40..0.58) bị trả về PASTE -> Click New lại bị Paste!
```
Ngoài ra:
- Khi người dùng click nút **+ New** trên Command Bar của Explorer (thanh lệnh trên cùng), hàm `_cursor_in_explorer_command_bar` nhận diện toàn bộ thanh lệnh là `[command-bar-paste]` mà không loại trừ nút `+ New` ở góc trái.
- Trong `_win32_menu_text_at_point`, hàm `MenuItemFromPoint` cho popup menu cổ điển `#32768` nhận toạ độ Client nhưng lại truyền toạ độ Screen khiến việc đọc chữ trên menu bị lệch.

---

### 2. Các chỉnh sửa đã thực hiện trong [filecopyclient.py](file:///d:/Data/AI%20Code/remote_desktop/filecopyclient.py)

*(Tuân thủ tuyệt đối yêu cầu: không sửa code bên [filecopyhost.py](file:///d:/Data/AI%20Code/remote_desktop/filecopyhost.py) và [windows_clipboard.py](file:///d:/Data/AI%20Code/remote_desktop/os_utils/windows_clipboard.py), chỉ chỉnh sửa logic bên [filecopyclient.py](file:///d:/Data/AI%20Code/remote_desktop/filecopyclient.py))*

1. **Sửa lại hình học phân loại menu Windows 11 (`_classify_xaml_menu_by_position`)**:
   - Thêm hàm `_is_desktop_context_target`: Tự động phân biệt context menu mở trên Desktop hay trong File Explorer.
   - **Trong File Explorer**: Dải icon trên cùng (`(pt.y - rc.top) <= top_band` hoặc `frac <= 0.22`) trả về `"paste"`. Toàn bộ thân menu bên dưới (View, Sort, Group, Refresh, **New**, Terminal...) trả về `"not_paste"`. Mục **New** sẽ **không bao giờ** bị nhận là Paste nữa.
   - **Trên Desktop**: Dải trên (View, Sort, Refresh) là `"not_paste"`, dải Paste/Paste shortcut (`frac 0.28..0.44`) là `"paste"`, dải New và bên dưới (`frac > 0.44`) chắc chắn là `"not_paste"`.
2. **Loại trừ nút "+ New" trên Command Bar (`_cursor_on_explorer_command_bar_new_button`)**:
   - Kiểm tra toạ độ và UIA text khi click trên Command Bar của Explorer. Nếu click vào nút `+ New` (vùng góc trái thanh lệnh) hoặc text trả về là token New/Mới thì trả về `"not_paste"`.
3. **Cải thiện đọc UIA (`_uia_menu_text_at_point`)**:
   - Quét cả `AutomationId`, `HelpText` ("Paste (Ctrl+V)", "Dán (Ctrl+V)") và `ItemType` để nhận diện các nút XAML dạng `AppBarButton` dù không có nhãn Text hiển thị.
4. **Sửa toạ độ `ScreenToClient` trong `_win32_menu_text_at_point`**:
   - Chuyển đổi toạ độ màn hình sang client coordinate trước khi gọi `MenuItemFromPoint`, đồng thời fallback qua `GetMenuItemInfoW` với `MIIM_STRING` để đọc tên mục menu `#32768` chính xác 100%.

214: def merge_full_service(app_dist_dir):
215:     """Ghép RemoteDesktopService.exe + deps thiếu vào app.dist (giống slim merge của Host)."""
216:     service_dist_dir = os.path.join(workspace_dir, "dist_nuitka_service", "windows_service_loop.dist")
217:     if not os.path.exists(service_dist_dir):
218:         return False
219:     svc_src = os.path.join(service_dist_dir, "windows_service_loop.exe")
220:     svc_dst = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, "RemoteDesktopService.exe")
221:     if not os.path.exists(svc_src):
222:         log("ERROR: windows_service_loop.exe missing in service dist.")
223:         return False
224:     log("Merging RemoteDesktopService.exe (slim — only missing runtime files)...")
225:     shutil.copy2(svc_src, svc_dst)
226:     for item in os.listdir(service_dist_dir):
227:         if item.lower() == "windows_service_loop.exe":
228:             continue
229:         s = os.path.join(service_dist_dir, item)
230:         d = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, item)
231:         if os.path.exists(d):
232:             continue
233:         try:
234:             if os.path.isdir(s):
235:                 shutil.copytree(s, d)
236:             else:
237:                 shutil.copy2(s, d)

239:             log(f"Warning: Failed to merge service file {item}: {e}")
240:     try_authenticode_sign(svc_dst)

245: def log_dist_size(app_dist_dir, label):
246:     try:
247:         total = 0
248:         for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(app_dist_dir):
249:             for name in files:
250:                 try:

252:                 except Exception:
253:                     pass
254:         log(f"{label}: {total / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB")
255:     except Exception as e:
256:         log(f"Could not measure {label}: {e}")

262:             log(f"{label}: {os.path.getsize(path) / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB ({path})")

267: def shorten_openblas_dll(app_dist_dir):
268:     """Đổi tên DLL OpenBLAS do NumPy đóng gói (tên hash dài) thành libopenblas.dll và vá import trong .pyd."""
269:     import re
270:     short_name = "libopenblas.dll"
271:     long_path = None
272:     long_name = None
273:     try:
274:         names = os.listdir(app_dist_dir)
275:     except Exception as e:
276:         log(f"Không đọc được {app_dist_dir}: {e}")
277:         return
278:     for name in names:
279:         if re.match(r"libopenblas\..+\.dll$", name, re.I) and name.lower() != short_name:
280:             long_path = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, name)
281:             long_name = name
282:             break
283:     if not long_path:
284:         log("No hashed libopenblas DLL found to rename.")
285:         return
286:     old_bytes = long_name.encode("ascii")
287:     short_bytes = short_name.encode("ascii")
288:     if len(short_bytes) >= len(old_bytes):
289:         log(f"Short name {short_name} is not shorter; skip.")
290:         return
291:     padded = short_bytes + b"\x00" * (len(old_bytes) - len(short_bytes))
292:     patched = 0
293:     for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(app_dist_dir):
294:         for f in files:
295:             if not f.lower().endswith((".dll", ".pyd", ".exe")):
296:                 continue
297:             path = os.path.join(root, f)
298:             try:
299:                 with open(path, "rb") as fh:
300:                     data = fh.read()
301:             except Exception:
302:                 continue
303:             if old_bytes not in data:
304:                 continue
305:             data = data.replace(old_bytes, padded)
306:             with open(path, "wb") as fh:
307:                 fh.write(data)
308:             patched += 1
309:             log(f"Patched OpenBLAS import in {os.path.relpath(path, app_dist_dir)}")
310:     dest = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, short_name)
311:     if os.path.abspath(dest) != os.path.abspath(long_path):
312:         if os.path.exists(dest):
313:             try:
314:                 os.remove(dest)
315:             except Exception as e:
316:                 log(f"Could not remove old {short_name}: {e}")
317:                 return
318:         os.rename(long_path, dest)
319:     log(f"Renamed {long_name} -> {short_name} (patched {patched} files).")
320: 
321: def try_authenticode_sign(exe_path):
322:     """Ký Authenticode nếu máy có signtool + chứng chỉ code signing (cách đúng để giảm Defender ML)."""
323:     if not os.path.exists(exe_path):
324:         return
325:     signtool = None
326:     kit = os.path.join(os.environ.get("ProgramFiles(x86)", r"C:\Program Files (x86)"), "Windows Kits", "10", "bin")
327:     if os.path.isdir(kit):
328:         for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(kit):
329:             if "signtool.exe" in files and os.path.basename(root).lower() == "x64":
330:                 signtool = os.path.join(root, "signtool.exe")
331:     if not signtool:
332:         log("Không tìm thấy signtool.exe — bỏ qua ký Authenticode. Defender ML có thể vẫn cảnh báo installer chưa ký.")
333:         log("Cách xử lý: chứng chỉ code signing, hoặc gửi file lên https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/wdsi/filesubmission")
334:         return
335:     cmd = f'"{signtool}" sign /a /fd SHA256 /tr http://timestamp.digicert.com /td SHA256 "{exe_path}"'
336:     if run_cmd(cmd):
337:         log(f"Đã ký Authenticode: {exe_path}")
338:     else:
339:         log("Không ký được (chưa có chứng chỉ code signing). Gửi Microsoft WDSI nếu Defender cảnh báo.")
340:

342:     main()
